Motivation: 现有文献对AI系统文化能力的定义模糊、术语混用，且通常仅限于回忆各种人口统计信息、地区和国家的准确事实，缺乏对文化能力的结构化理解，这可能导致评估结果高估模型能力，并在文化敏感背景下做出不当部署决策。

Method: 借鉴跨文化传播学（Intercultural Communication）学术成果，提出一个三层次的AI相关文化能力分类法：文化意识（Cultural Awareness）回答"模型知道吗？"，文化敏感性（Cultural Sensitivity）回答"它如何框架化其知识？"，文化能力（Cultural Competence）回答"它能随着交互演进进行适应吗？"。

Result: 提出了一个结构化的三层分类法来澄清AI文化能力的概念模糊性，并将该分类法定位为提升AI在真实多元文化环境中评估有效性和可解释性的实用工具。

Conclusion: 该分类法有助于解决AI文化能力评估中的构念模糊性问题，避免评估结果高估模型能力，从而防止在文化敏感场景中的不当部署决策，促进更严谨、更具解释力的AI文化能力评估。

Abstract: Tremendous efforts have been put into evaluating the inclusivity and effectiveness of AI systems across cultures. However, the cultural capabilities considered in much of the literature remain vaguely defined, are referred to using interchangeable terminology, and are typically limited to recalling accurate information about various demographics, regions, and nationalities. To address this construct ambiguity, we draw from Intercultural Communication scholarship and propose a three-level taxonomy of AI-relevant cultural capabilities: Cultural Awareness answers "Does the model know?", Cultural Sensitivity answers "How does it frame its knowledge?", and Cultural Competence answers "Can it adapt as the interaction evolves?". Beyond conceptual clarification, we position this taxonomy as a practical tool for improving the validity and interpretability of AI evaluation in real-world, multicultural settings. Without such construct clarity, evaluation results risk overstating model capabilities and may lead to inappropriate deployment decisions in culturally sensitive contexts.

</details>


### [113] [Can Vision Language Models Be Adaptive in Mathematics Education? A Learner Model-based Rubric Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16011)
*Jie Gao,Yongan Yu,Junzhu Su,Yiran Lin,Adam K. Dube,Jackie Chi Kit Cheung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对当前视觉语言模型(VLM)在数学辅导中缺乏对不同学习者画像自适应能力评估的问题，借鉴自适应学习框架中的学习者模型，提出了一个包含认知、动机和复杂性三个方面的自适应性评估指标体系，实验表明现有VLM在生成基于学习者模型的教学响应方面存在困难，尤其在学习者信息有限时表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM已被用于数学教育作为个性化学习辅助工具，但缺乏系统评估框架来衡量它们能否根据不同学习者画像（如认知水平、动机状态等）自适应地调整数学教学指令。

Method: 借鉴自适应学习框架中的学习者模型(Shute and Towle, 2018)，提出基于学习者模型的评估量规，形式化自适应性评估为三个维度：认知方面、动机方面和复杂性；额外评估回答正确性和响应质量两个维度。

Result: 实验结果显示不同模型在自适应性上存在可测量差异，当前VLM难以一致地生成基于学习者模型的教学响应，尤其在接收有限学习者信息时表现更差。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在数学辅导任务中对不同学习者画像的自适应能力不足，需要进一步改进以更好地支持个性化教学。

Abstract: Adaptive learning refers to educational technologies that track learners' learning progress and adapt the instructional process based on individual learners' learning performance. It is increasingly recognized as critical for developing an effective learning support tool. Vision language models (VLMs) have seen adoption in mathematics education, and students have been using them as learning aids for personalized instruction. However, it is unknown whether VLMs have the ability to adapt to different learner profiles when providing mathematical instructions. Current VLMs lack a systematic evaluation framework for this adaptivity to different learner profiles in mathematics tutoring tasks. To address this gap, we draw on the learner model from the adaptive learning framework (Shute and Towle, 2018) and propose a learner model-based rubric. Our rubric formalizes adaptivity assessment into three aspects: cognitive aspects, motivational aspects, and complexity. We also evaluate two additional dimensions of VLM responses: correctness (of answers and solutions) and quality (of the response itself). Our experimental results show measurable differences in adaptivity across models and also reveal that current VLMs struggle to consistently produce learner model-based instructional responses, especially when receiving limited learner information.

</details>


### [114] [Judge Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16023)
*Nils Feldhus,Tanja Baeumel,Elena Golimblevskaia,Qianli Wang,Van Bach Nguyen,Aaron Louis Eidt,Christopher Ebert,Wojciech Samek,Jing Yang,Vera Schmitt,Sebastian Möller,Simon Ostermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过因果分析发现，LLM作为评判者时，不同输出格式（如1-5评分 vs True/False）导致的评分不一致，其根源在于模型内部存在一个共享的"潜在评估器"子图（位于中后层MLP），而格式特定的终端分支在映射连续判断信号时不稳定，从而解释了格式诱导的不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅从输入输出层面诊断LLM作为评判者时因输出格式变化导致的评分不一致问题（如1-5评分 vs 真/假标签），缺乏对模型内部机制的因果理解。本文旨在从机制层面揭示格式诱导不一致性的根本原因。

Method: 使用位置感知边归因补丁（PEAP）方法，对Gemma-3、Qwen2.5和Llama-3三个开源模型进行因果机制分析，识别与评判相关的内部子图，并通过零消融实验验证其因果作用。

Result: 发现结构理解与开放式偏好任务中的判断共享一个稀疏、泛化的"潜在评估器"子图，位于中到后期MLP层；零消融该子图会破坏评判能力但保留世界知识。判断信号在共享主干中连续计算，通过脆弱且格式特定的终端分支映射到输出，导致格式依赖性不一致。

Conclusion: 格式诱导的不一致性源于模型架构中判断信号计算与输出格式映射的机械解耦，意味着跨格式的基准可靠性比较部分衡量的是格式化器几何特性而非评估质量。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge has become the dominant paradigm for grading model outputs at scale, yet the same model assigns systematically different scores when its output format changes (e.g., a 1-5 rating vs. a True/False label). Existing diagnoses of these format-induced inconsistencies stop at the input-output level. Using Position-aware Edge Attribution Patching (PEAP), we causally investigate the internal mechanism in Gemma-3, Qwen2.5, and Llama-3. We find that judgments across structured understanding and open-ended preference tasks share a sparse, generalized Latent Evaluator sub-graph in the mid-to-late multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs); zero-ablating it collapses judgment while preserving world knowledge in architecturally modular models. By structurally decoupling abstract judging from output formatting, we provide a mechanistic account of format-induced inconsistency on the open-weight models we study: a continuous judgment signal computed in the shared trunk is mapped through fragile, format-specific terminal branches, enabling format-independent preference to be isolated downstream of the requested output format. Our findings imply that benchmark-level reliability comparisons across formats are partially measuring formatter geometry rather than evaluation quality.

</details>


### [115] [From Flat Language Labels to Typological Priors: Structured Language Conditioning for Multilingual Speech-to-Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16026)
*Yu Pan,Yang Hou,Xiongfei Wu,Liang Zhang,Yves Le Traon,Lei Ma,Jianjun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了S2ST-Omni 2，一种将多语言条件从扁平标签重构为结构化类型学先验的组成式语音到语音翻译框架，在CVSS-C上取得了优异性能，并在低资源场景下展现了数据高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有S2ST系统要么忽略源语言信息，要么通过"语言即标签"范式将其编码为独立的扁平嵌入，忽视了跨语言的系统性语言结构，限制了监督数据稀缺时的数据高效多语言自适应能力。

Method: S2ST-Omni 2在三个层级重构语言条件：类型学引导的分层语言编码（表示级）、动态门控语言感知Dual-CTC（声学级）、类型学感知LLM提示（解码级），将扁平语言标签替换为结构化类型学先验。

Result: 在CVSS-C上，S2ST-Omni 2在BLEU、COMET、ASR-BLEU和BLASER 2.0指标上优于代表性S2ST方法；消融实验验证了三层策略的互补性；仅用约3小时日语-英语监督数据即展现了类型学先验对数据高效多语言S2ST的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式的类型学先验为数据高效的多语言S2ST提供了有用的归纳偏置，三层策略（表示级、声学级、解码级）具有互补优势。

Abstract: Compositional speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) systems built upon speech large language models (SpeechLLMs) have recently shown promising performance. However, existing S2ST systems often either neglect source-language information or encode it through a language-as-label paradigm, representing each source language as an independent flat embedding. Such a design overlooks systematic linguistic structure shared across languages, which may limit data-efficient multilingual adaptation when supervised S2ST data are scarce. To address this issue, we propose S2ST-Omni 2, a many-to-one compositional S2ST framework that systematically reformulates multilingual language conditioning from flat language labels to structured typological priors. Specifically, S2ST-Omni 2 revisits language conditioning at three levels: typology-informed hierarchical language encoding for structured source-language representation, dynamically-gated language-aware Dual-CTC for content-adaptive acoustic modulation, and typology-aware LLM prompting for decoder-side linguistic guidance. Experiments on CVSS-C show that S2ST-Omni 2 achieves superior average performance among representative S2ST approaches across BLEU, COMET, ASR-BLEU, and BLASER 2.0 under the adopted evaluation protocol. Ablation studies indicate that the proposed representation-level, acoustic-level, and decoding-level strategies provide complementary benefits. Moreover, controlled data-budget analyses and a Japanese-to-English evaluation using only approximately 3 hours of supervised training data suggest that explicit typological priors provide useful inductive biases for data-efficient multilingual S2ST.

</details>


### [116] [RecMem: Recurrence-based Memory Consolidation for Efficient and Effective Long-Running LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16045)
*Zijie Dai,Shiyuan Deng,Sheng Guan,Yizhou Tian,Xin Yao,Xiao Yan,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RecMem通过引入基于重复性触发的记忆整合机制，将交互信息先存储在潜意识记忆层并用轻量级嵌入模型编码，仅在检测到语义相似交互的持续重复时才调用LLM提取情景记忆和语义记忆，从而在降低高达87%的token消耗的同时提升记忆系统准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统对每次交互都调用LLM进行记忆提取（急切记忆整合），导致大量token消耗，亟需一种更高效的记忆整合策略。

Method: 1) 引入潜意识记忆层，用轻量级嵌入模型编码存储交互；2) 基于重复性检测机制，仅在语义相似交互持续出现时才调用LLM提取情景记忆和语义记忆；3) 语义精炼机制恢复记忆提取中遗漏的细粒度事实。

Result: 在三种SOTA记忆系统上，RecMem将记忆构建的token成本降低高达87%，同时准确性超过原系统。

Conclusion: 基于重复性触发的记忆整合策略能大幅降低token消耗并提升准确性，证明了并非所有交互都需要立即进行LLM级别的记忆提取。

Abstract: Memory systems often organize user-agent interactions as retrievable external memory and are crucial for long-running agents by overcoming the limited context windows of LLMs. However, existing memory systems invoke LLMs to process every incoming interaction for memory extraction, and such an eager memory consolidation scheme leads to substantial token consumption. To tackle this problem, we propose RecMem by rethinking when memory consolidation should be conducted. RecMem stores incoming interactions in a subconscious memory layer and encode them using lightweight embedding models for retrieval. LLMs are only invoked to extract episodic and semantic memory when sustained recurrence are observed for semantically similar interactions. Such recurrence-based consolidation works because these interactions correspond to a semantic cluster with rich information and thus are worth extraction and summarization. To improve accuracy, RecMem also incorporates a semantic refinement mechanism that recovers the fine-grained facts omitted by memory extraction. Experiments show that RecMem reduces the memory construction token cost of three SOTA memory systems by up to 87% while exceeding their accuracy.

</details>


### [117] [Can Large Language Models Imitate Human Speech for Clinical Assessment? LLM-Driven Data Augmentation for Cognitive Score Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16077)
*Si-Belkacem Yamine Ketir,Lenard Paulo Tamayo,Shohei Hisada,Shaowen Peng,Shoko Wakamiya,Eiji Aramaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（GPT-5）驱动的数据增强框架，利用书面回答作为语义锚点生成多种风格的口语式独白，并结合相似性引导的类别平衡选择策略，显著提升了从自发语音中预测认知评分（Hasegawa痴呆量表）的性能，尤其改善了少数低分样本的预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自发语音中准确评估认知衰退仍面临数据集规模小和类别不平衡的挑战，现有方法难以有效处理这些问题。

Method: 使用GPT-5基于书面回答生成多种风格的口语式独白进行数据增强；采用Sentence-BERT提取语音嵌入；通过Partial Least Squares回归预测Hasegawa痴呆量表得分；提出两种增强策略：随机类别平衡选择和相似性引导的类别平衡选择。

Result: 随机类别平衡选择带来中等但不稳定的改进；相似性引导的类别平衡选择实现了更一致的改进，显著降低了少数低分参与者的预测误差，同时保持了多数群体的性能。

Conclusion: 语义引导的LLM驱动数据增强是解决临床语音分析中类别不平衡和提高数据效率的一种有原则的有效方法。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of cognitive decline from spontaneous speech remains challenging due to limited dataset size and class imbalance. In this work, we propose a large language model (LLM)-driven data augmentation framework to improve the prediction of cognitive scores from speech. Experiments are conducted on a Japanese corpus in which each participant provides both a spontaneous oral narrative and a written response to the same clinical prompt. The written responses serve as semantic anchors to generate multiple oral-like monologues in different styles using GPT-5. We then predict Hasegawa Dementia Scale scores, a widely used cognitive screening tool in Japan, using a Partial Least Squares regression model trained on Sentence-BERT speech embeddings. We investigate two augmentation strategies: random class-balanced selection, which yields moderate but unstable improvements, and similarity-guided class-balanced selection. The latter prioritizes semantically close synthetic samples, leading to more consistent improvements and substantially reducing prediction error for minority low-score participants while maintaining performance for the majority group. Overall, our findings demonstrate the potential of semantically guided LLM-driven augmentation as a principled approach for addressing class imbalance and improving data efficiency in clinical speech analysis.

</details>


### [118] [DebiasRAG: A Tuning-Free Path to Fair Generation in Large Language Models through Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16113)
*Rui Chu,Bingyin Zhao,Thanh Quoc Hung Le,Duy Cao Hoang,Huawei Lin,Ping Li,Weijie Zhao,Khoa D Doan,Yingjie Lao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DebiasRAG，一种基于检索增强生成的无需微调、动态查询特定的去偏框架，通过三个阶段（查询特定去偏候选生成、上下文候选池构建、梯度更新的去偏引导上下文片段重排序）减少LLM中的社会偏见，同时保持模型原有能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs存在社会偏见（种族、性别、年龄等），现有微调和提示工程方法需要额外训练资源或领域知识，可能降低模型原始能力，且缺乏动态去偏上下文支持。

Method: DebiasRAG包含三个阶段：(1) 查询特定去偏候选生成：通过检索与查询相关的自诊断偏见上下文，反向生成去偏上下文；(2) 上下文候选池构建：从常规RAG文档库（如Wikipedia）检索与查询相关的上下文；(3) 梯度更新的去偏引导上下文片段重排序：利用梯度更新对上下文片段进行重排序，以强化去偏约束。

Result: DebiasRAG能够在无需微调的情况下实现动态去偏，提升公平性，同时保持LLM的固有表示能力和生成能力。

Conclusion: DebiasRAG是一种无需额外训练、动态查询特定的去偏框架，通过RAG机制有效缓解LLM社会偏见，且不损害模型原有性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved unprecedented success due to their exceptional generative capabilities. However, because they depend on knowledge encapsulated from training corpora, they may produce hallucinations, stereotypes, and socially biased content. In particular, LLMs are prone to prejudiced responses involving race, gender, and age, which are collectively referred to as social biases. Prior studies have used fine-tuning and prompt engineering to mitigate such biases in LLMs, but these methods require additional training resources or domain knowledge to design the framework. Moreover, they may degrade the original capabilities of LLMs and often overlook the need for dynamic debiasing contexts for fairer inference. In this paper, we propose DebiasRAG, a novel tuning-free and dynamic query-specific debiasing framework based on retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). DebiasRAG improves fairness while preserving the intrinsic properties of LLMs, such as representation ability. DebiasRAG consists of three stages: (1) query-specific debiasing candidate generation; (2) context candidate pool construction; and (3) gradient-updated debiasing-guided context piece reranking. First, DebiasRAG leverages self-diagnosed bias contexts relevant to the query through regular retrieval, where the bias contexts are prepared offline by the DebiasRAG provider. Given the query-specific bias contexts, DebiasRAG reversely produces debiasing contexts, which are provided as additional fairness constraints for LLM outputs. Second, a regular RAG retrieval process produces query-related contexts from the regular RAG document database, such as a chunked Wikipedia dataset.

</details>


### [119] [SGR: A Stepwise Reasoning Framework for LLMs with External Subgraph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16117)
*Xin Zhang,Yang Cao,Baoxing Wu,Kai Song,Siying Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SGR框架，通过为每问题构建外部知识库的子图并引导LLM逐步推理，从而提升复杂推理任务的准确性与事实可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在需要深度推理和逻辑推断的复杂场景中仍存在局限，生成过程中容易引入无关、噪声或事实不一致的内容。

Method: SGR首先根据输入问题从外部知识库构建查询特定子图，然后引导模型在生成的子图上逐步推理，最后融合多条推理轨迹得到最终预测。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，SGR相较于强基线方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: SGR通过将中间推理步骤锚定在结构化外部知识上，有效提升了推理准确性和事实可靠性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities across diverse NLP applications, such as translation, text generation, and question answering. Nevertheless, they remain limited in complex settings that demand deep reasoning and logical inference. Since these models are trained on large-scale text corpora, their generation process may still introduce irrelevant, noisy, or factually inconsistent content. To mitigate this problem, we introduce SGR, a stepwise framework that enhances LLM reasoning through external subgraph generation. SGR builds query-specific subgraphs from external knowledge bases and uses their semantic structure to support multi-step inference. By grounding intermediate reasoning steps in structured external knowledge, the framework helps the model concentrate on relevant entities, relations, and supporting evidence. In particular, SGR first constructs a subgraph tailored to the input question. It then guides the model to reason progressively over the generated structure and combines multiple reasoning trajectories to obtain the final prediction. Experimental results across several benchmark datasets show that SGR achieves consistent improvements over competitive baselines, highlighting its value for improving both reasoning accuracy and factual reliability.

</details>


### [120] [Artificial Aphasias in Lesioned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16222)
*Nathan Roll,Jill Kries,Laura Gwilliams,Cory Shain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受失语症启发的技术，通过“损伤”（归零）大语言模型参数并测量其对临床症状的影响，来表征语言模型的 emergent 功能组织。研究发现，尽管模型能表现出全部失语症症状，但其分布与人类显著不同，且症状模式受学习与处理细节的影响，而非领域不变的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用失语症（脑损伤导致的选择性语言障碍）提供的脑区与症状之间的因果联系，将其作为理论框架来揭示语言模型中功能组织的涌现特性。

Method: 对五个10亿参数级别的语言模型进行“损伤”（将参数归零），然后通过文本失语症评估量表（TAB）测量损伤后的模型输出，将其与临床失语症症状进行对照分析。

Result: 模型表现出全部失语症症状但分布与人类不同；注意力组件与前馈组件之间存在广泛的症状谱差异，而同一机制内组件差异较弱；早期层损伤主要导致句法和语义症状，中后期层损伤导致音韵和流畅性障碍。

Conclusion: 虽然某些模型损伤在定量上与某些人类失语症类型更相似，但定性的症状模式差异表明，失语症综合征在很大程度上受学习与处理细节的影响，而非语言处理中断的领域不变结果。

Abstract: Aphasias, selective language impairments which can arise from brain damage, reveal the functional organization of human language by providing causal links between affected brain regions and specific symptom profiles. Drawing on this literature, we introduce an aphasia-inspired technique to characterize the emergent functional organization of language models (LMs). We ``lesion'' (zero-out) model parameters and measure the effects of this intervention against clinical aphasia symptoms, as diagnosed by the Text Aphasia Battery (TAB). When applied to 112,426 outputs from five 1B-scale LMs, the full range of evaluated symptoms surface, but in distributions largely distinct from those of humans. Our method uncovers broad symptom-profile differences between attention components (query, key, value, output) and feed-forward components (up, gate, down), with weaker evidence for differences among components within the same mechanism. We also find an effect of depth, where lesions in early layers disproportionately cause syntactic and semantic symptoms while late-middle layers yield higher rates of phonological and fluency deficits. Although some LM lesions induce quantitatively more similar profiles to some human aphasia types than others, qualitative differences in symptom patterns between LMs and humans suggest that aphasia syndromes are heavily influenced by the details of learning and processing rather than being a domain-invariant consequence of disrupted language processing.

</details>


### [121] [A Unified Generative-AI Framework for Smart Energy Infrastructure: Intelligent Gas Distribution, Utility Billing, Carbon Analytics, and Quantum-Inspired Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16232)
*Pavan Manjunath,Thomas pruefer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了智能计量、生成式人工智能和量子启发组合优化三者融合如何重塑能源公用事业在基础设施管理、客户参与和环境责任方面的实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对能源行业在物理资产管理、用户交互和环保合规方面日益复杂的需求，探索新兴技术（智能计量、生成式AI、量子启发优化）的协同效应与整合路径。

Method: 概念性分析与技术综述，聚焦三种技术（智能计量、生成式AI、量子启发组合优化）的交叉融合框架。

Result: 指出三者的融合能够加速能源公用事业在基础设施管理、客户互动和环保责任三大维度的转型。

Conclusion: 智能计量、生成式AI与量子启发优化的协同是推动能源行业实现高效、智能、可持续转型的关键驱动力。

Abstract: The accelerating convergence of smart metering, generative artificial intelligence, and quantum-inspired combinatorial optimisation is reshaping how energy utilities manage physical infrastructure, customer engagement, and environmental accountability

</details>


### [122] [A Generative AI Framework for Intelligent Utility Billing CO 2 Analytics and Sustainable Resource Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16250)
*Pavan Manjunath,Thomas Pruefer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个端到端框架，统一了四种生产级能力：基于生成式AI的自然语言账单生成、变压器模型负荷预测、碳排放核算和负荷调度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电公用事业公司需要提供用户可读的账单、为每千瓦时电力附加可辩护的碳排放数据，并基于电网压力和排放约束调度负荷。

Method: 提出了一个统一四种能力的端到端框架：1）基于受限解码策略的生成式AI智能体将结构化数值输入转换为自然语言账单；2）变压器模型提供日前消费估计及校准分位数区间。

Result: 初步描述了框架的组成部分，未提供具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 该框架有望实现账单可读性、碳排放核算可信度和基于约束的负荷调度的统一。

Abstract: Distribution utilities are now expected to deliver bills that customers can actually read attach a defensible carbon number to every kWh sold and schedule load against grid stress and emissions constraints We propose an end-to-end framework that unifies four production-grade capabilities under one architectural roof a generative-AI agent that drafts each customers natural-language billing statement from structured numeric inputs under a constrained decoding policy a transformer-based forecaster that supplies the day-ahead consumption estimate with calibrated quantile bands

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [DeepSlide: From Artifacts to Presentation Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15202)
*Ming Yang,Zhiwei Zhang,Jiahang Li,Haoseng Liu,Yuzheng Cai,Weiguo Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSlide是一个人在循环的多智能体系统，通过可控逻辑链规划、内容树检索、马尔可夫顺序渲染和沙箱执行，从需求获取到排练支持全流程优化演示文稿的生成与交付质量，在保持幻灯片质量的同时显著提升叙事流畅性、节奏精确性和讲稿协同性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI幻灯片生成器主要优化视觉外观（幻灯片本身），而忽略了演示交付过程（节奏、叙事和演讲准备）的优化，导致生成的幻灯片在实际演讲中效果不佳。

Method: DeepSlide采用人在循环的多智能体系统架构，包含四个核心模块：(i) 可控逻辑链规划器（带节点时间预算），(ii) 轻量级内容树检索器（用于事实依据），(iii) 马尔可夫风格顺序渲染（带样式继承），(iv) 沙箱化执行与最小修复机制（确保可渲染性）。同时引入双记分板基准，将静态幻灯片质量与动态交付效果分离评估。

Result: 在20个领域和多种受众画像上，DeepSlide在幻灯片质量指标上与强基线持平，同时在交付指标上取得更大增益，包括叙事流畅性、节奏精确性、讲稿协同性以及注意力引导清晰度均有提升。

Conclusion: DeepSlide证明了在幻灯片生成中同时关注静态质量和动态交付效果的重要性，通过全流程优化实现了更好的演示效果，为AI辅助演示系统设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Presentations are a primary medium for scholarly communication, yet most AI slide generators optimize the artifact (a visually plausible deck) while under-optimizing the delivery process (pacing, narrative, and presentation preparation). We present DeepSlide, a human-in-the-loop multi-agent system that supports preparing the full presentation process, from requirement elicitation and time-budgeted narrative planning, to evidence-grounded slide--script generation, attention augmentation, and rehearsal support. DeepSlide integrates (i) a controllable logical-chain planner with per-node time budgets, (ii) a lightweight content-tree retriever for grounding, (iii) Markov-style sequential rendering with style inheritance, and (iv) sandboxed execution with minimal repair to ensure renderability. We further introduce a dual-scoreboard benchmark that cleanly separates static artifact quality from dynamic delivery excellence. Across 20 domains and diverse audience profiles, DeepSlide matches strong baselines on artifact quality while consistently achieving larger gains on delivery metrics, improving narrative flow, pacing precision, and slide--script synergy with clearer attention guidance.

</details>


### [124] [SkillSmith: Compiling Agent Skills into Boundary-Guided Runtime Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15215)
*Duling Xu,Zheng Chen,Zaifeng Pan,Jiawei Guan,Dong Dong,Jialin Li,Bangzheng Pu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillSmith提出了一种边界优先的编译器-运行时框架，通过从技能中提取细粒度操作边界，将技能包离线编译为最小可执行接口，从而减少LLM Agent系统中的冗余上下文注入和重复推理规划，在SkillsBench上实现了57.44%的token节省、42.99%的思考迭代减少、50.57%的求解时间缩短以及57.44%的成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent系统中，技能通常以完整上下文形式注入推理循环，导致两个主要冗余来源：无关上下文注入和重复的技能特定推理与规划。本文旨在解决这些冗余问题，提升Agent系统的效率。

Method: 提出SkillSmith框架，采用边界优先的编译器-运行时架构：离线阶段将技能包编译为最小可执行接口，提取细粒度操作边界；运行时阶段Agent仅动态访问和执行相关组件，避免不必要的上下文注入和冗余推理开销。

Result: 在SkillsBench基准上，相比使用原始技能，求解阶段token使用量减少57.44%，思考迭代次数减少42.99%，求解时间减少50.57%（加速2.02倍），按token计费的成本降低57.44%。此外，强模型编译的产物可被小模型复用，在原始技能解释失败时提升任务准确率。

Conclusion: SkillSmith通过边界优先的编译-运行时框架，有效消除了技能使用中的冗余，显著提升了LLM Agent系统的效率，且编译产物具有跨模型迁移能力，为技能管理与复用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recently, skills have been widely adopted in large language model (LLM)-based agent systems across various domains. In existing frameworks, skills are typically injected into the agent reasoning loop as contextual guidance once matched to a runtime task, enabling specialized task-solving capabilities. We find that this execution paradigm introduces two major sources of redundancy: irrelevant context injection and repeated skill-specific reasoning and planning. To this end, we propose SkillSmith, a boundary-first compiler-runtime framework that compiles skill packages offline into minimal executable interfaces. By extracting fine-grained operational boundaries from skills, SkillSmith enables agents to dynamically access and execute only the relevant components at runtime, thereby minimizing unnecessary context injection and redundant reasoning overhead. In the evaluation on SkillsBench benchmark, SkillSmith reduces solve-stage token usage by 57.44%, thinking iterations by 42.99%, solve time by 50.57% (2.02x faster), and token-proportional monetary cost by 57.44% compared with using raw-skills. Moreover, compiled artifacts produced by a stronger model can be reused by a smaller or more efficient runtime model, improving task accuracy in cases where raw skill interpretation fails. The source code and data are available at https://github.com/AetherHeart-AI/Aeloon.

</details>


### [125] [Fair outputs, Biased Internals: Causal Potency and Asymmetry of Latent Bias in LLMs for High-Stakes Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15217)
*Jagdish Tripathy,Marcus Buckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 指令微调的语言模型在高风险决策中表现出行为公平性，但内部表征仍保留偏见关联。研究发现，这些被抑制的表征通过激活引导和跨层干预可导致决策逆转，且这种潜在偏见具有不对称性，仅影响特定人口统计方向，并可被对抗性提示和参数高效微调利用。这表明仅关注输出的行为审计不足，需要结合输出评估与表征分析的双层测试框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究指令微调语言模型中被抑制的偏见表征是否仍然具有因果效力（能够影响模型输出），以及这种因果效力在不同人口统计群体间是否对称。现有研究仅关注了行为层面的公平性，忽视了内部表征中残留偏见可能被利用的风险。

Method: 使用开放权重模型处理抵押贷款承保任务，采用匹配仅种族相关姓名不同的贷款申请；通过激活引导和跨层干预技术，在关键层级重新注入被抑制的偏见信息，观察决策逆转程度；测试偏见在不同人口统计方向上的不对称性；评估对抗性提示工程和参数高效微调对潜藏偏见的利用效果。

Result: 模型在输出层面无偏见，但内部表征保留并放大了人口统计表征；重新注入偏见信息可导致近乎完全的决策逆转；该潜在偏见具有不对称性（仅影响单方向决策），且易受对抗性提示和参数高效微调的攻击。

Conclusion: 仅关注输出的行为审计不足以保证模型公平性，公平输出可能掩盖可被利用的内部偏见；需要结合输出评估与表征分析的"双层测试"框架用于AI治理。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models exhibit behavioural fairness in high-stakes decisions while retaining biased associations in their internal representations. However, whether these suppressed representations can affect model outputs - and whether such causal potency is symmetric across demographic groups - remains unknown. We investigate the use of open-weight models for mortgage underwriting using matched applications that differ only in racially-associated names and reveal a critical disconnect: models show no output-level bias, yet retain and amplify demographic representations across model layers. Through activation steering and novel cross-layer interventions, we demonstrate that this suppressed information is decision-relevant: when reinjected at critical layers, it produces near-complete decision reversals. Critically, this latent bias is asymmetric - steering interventions affect decisions in one demographic direction, while producing minimal effects in reverse - and susceptible to adversarial prompt engineering and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. These findings demonstrate that behavioural audits focused on outputs are insufficient: fair outputs can mask exploitable internal biases. They also motivate dual-layer testing frameworks combining output evaluation with representational analysis for AI governance in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [126] [CAX-Agent: A Lightweight Agent Harness for Reliable APDL Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15218)
*Chenying Lin,Yichen Hai,Yi He,Ran Wang,Haiyan Qiang,Liang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAX-Agent轻量级智能体框架，通过三层架构（LLM服务层、智能体编排层、求解器后端层）和恢复阶梯策略解决大语言模型在MAPDL有限元仿真中的可靠性问题。实验表明，基于模型的恢复策略在任务完成率（0.9267）、任务得分（3.59/4）和零干预率（0.84）上显著优于无恢复和仅规则恢复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型直接部署于MAPDL有限元仿真时缺乏结构化执行控制、工具封装和故障恢复机制，导致输出不一致、任务频繁失败，亟需一种领域特定的编排中间件来管理工具生命周期、工作流状态和恢复升级。

Method: 提出CAX-Agent三层架构：LLM服务层、智能体编排层（核心为恢复阶梯，从确定性规则修补、模型驱动再生、上下文丰富到人工干预逐级升级）和求解器后端层。在50个标准结构基准上评估三种恢复策略（无恢复、仅规则、仅模型），每策略重复3轮共450次运行，由双盲独立评分者评估。

Result: 仅模型恢复策略在完成率（0.9267）、任务得分（3.59/4）、总分（9.16/10）和零干预率（0.84）上最优，优于仅规则恢复（0.7733, 3.17/4, 7.03/10, 0.00）和无恢复（0.6933, 2.74/4, 5.60/10, 0.00），效应量大（Cliff's delta 0.81-0.87），评分者间一致性高（Cohen's kappa=0.84）。

Conclusion: 模型驱动的恢复策略在MAPDL自动化仿真中显著优于规则恢复和无恢复策略，但基准使用简化几何以隔离恢复策略效应，结果泛化性需进一步验证。

Abstract: Large language models deployed for MAPDL finite-element simulation face practical reliability challenges: without structured execution control, tool encapsulation, and fault recovery, outputs may be inconsistent and task failures are common. The Agent Harness paradigm addresses this by inserting domain-specific orchestration middleware that manages tool lifecycles, workflow state, and recovery escalation. This paper presents the architecture of CAX-Agent, a lightweight agent harness purpose-built for MAPDL automation, and empirically evaluates one of its core components -- the recovery policy.CAX-Agent organizes execution into three layers -- LLM service, agent harness, and solver backend -- with a recovery ladder that escalates from deterministic rule patching through model-driven regeneration to context enrichment and human intervention. We evaluate three recovery strategies (no_recovery, rule_only, and model_only) on 50 standard structural benchmarks with three repeated runs per strategy (450 case-runs total). Two independent human raters score task completion under blind conditions; inter-rater agreement is strong (quadratic weighted Cohen's kappa = 0.84, 96 percent of score pairs within one point). Model_only achieves the best completion rate (0.9267), task score (3.59/4), total score (9.16/10), and zero-intervention rate (0.84), outperforming rule_only (0.7733, 3.17/4, 7.03/10, 0.00) and no_recovery (0.6933, 2.74/4, 5.60/10, 0.00) with large effect sizes (Cliff's delta = 0.81-0.87). The benchmark uses deliberately simple geometries to isolate recovery-policy effects; we discuss the scope of these findings and directions for broader validation.

</details>


### [127] [NOVA: Fundamental Limits of Knowledge Discovery Through AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15219)
*Salman Avestimehr,Ken Duffy,Muriel Médard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NOVA框架，将AI系统的自我提升循环建模为知识空间上的自适应采样过程，分析了知识发现的成功条件、失败模式、污染陷阱，并证明了在Zipf定律假设下发现成本服从Θ(c_gen D^α)的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统能否通过迭代自我改进发现真正的新知识，以及这种发现所需付出的代价。

Method: 提出NOVA框架，将"生成-验证-积累-重训练"循环建模为知识空间上的自适应采样过程；使用Good-Turing估计作为局部批量多样性诊断工具；在尾等价假设下建立Zipf标度律分析。

Result: 识别了四种失败模式：污染、遗忘、探索失败和接受失败；发现污染陷阱——当易发现知识耗尽时，小假阳性率会导致无效知识更快积累；证明发现成本服从Θ(c_gen D^α)的标度律。

Conclusion: 阐明了AI自主知识发现的理论边界和成本规律，揭示了人类专家指导在自主探索屏障处价值最大化的原因。

Abstract: Can AI systems discover genuinely new knowledge through iterative self improvement, and if so, at what cost? We introduce the NOVA framework, which models the common ``generate, verify, accumulate, retrain'' loop as an adaptive sampling process over a knowledge space. We identify sufficient conditions under which accumulated genuine knowledge eventually covers a finite domain, and show how their violations produce distinct failure modes: contamination, forgetting, exploration failure, and acceptance failure. We then analyze imperfect verification and identify a contamination trap: as easy-to-find knowledge is exhausted, the model mass assigned to new valid artifacts shrinks, so even small false-positive rates can cause invalid artifacts to enter the knowledge base faster than genuine discoveries. We clarify that Good--Turing estimation is a local batch-diversity diagnostic, not an estimator of the historically undiscovered valid mass that governs long-term discovery. Under a separate tail-equivalence assumption relating the model's effective discovery distribution to a Zipf law with exponent $α>1$, we prove that the cumulative generation cost required to obtain $D$ distinct genuine discoveries satisfies $R_{\mathrm{cum}}(D)=Θ(c_{\mathrm{gen}}D^α)$, where $c_{\mathrm{gen}}$ is the per-candidate generation cost. This scaling law quantifies asymptotic diminishing returns as the discovery frontier advances. Finally, we formalize human amplification through guidance, generation, and verification, explaining why expert input is most valuable near autonomous exploration barriers.

</details>


### [128] [Zero-Shot Goal Recognition with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15333)
*Kin Max Piamolini Gusmão,Nathan Gavenski,Nir Oren,Felipe Meneguzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地在零样本设置下评估了前沿大语言模型在经典PDDL规划领域中的目标识别能力。结果表明，部分模型能够随证据积累提升准确率并接近地标方法，而另一些模型则固守世界知识先验，无法有效整合证据。定性分析显示，这种差异源于证据整合能力而非领域熟悉度。目标识别可作为评估LLM规划基础知识的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在经典规划任务上已接近传统规划器的表现，但这种能力主要依赖世界知识而非真正的符号推理。目标识别作为一种溯因推理任务，本质上更适合评估LLM的规划基础知识，但目前缺乏系统性的零样本评估。

Method: 在经典PDDL基准领域上，对前沿LLM进行零样本目标识别评估，比较其与地标方法（landmark-based）的准确性，并对模型推理轨迹进行定性分析。

Result: LLM在目标识别上的表现不均衡：部分模型随证据增加准确率提升，接近地标方法在完整观测下的水平；另一些模型无论证据多少都固守世界知识先验。

Conclusion: 模型表现差异的根本原因是证据整合能力不同而非领域熟悉度差异，目标识别可作为评估LLM规划基础知识的原理性基准。

Abstract: Large language models have recently reached near-parity with classical planners on well-known planning domains, yet this competence relies on world-knowledge exploitation rather than genuine symbolic reasoning. Goal recognition is a complementary abductive task structurally better suited to LLM strengths: it consists of evaluating consistency with world knowledge rather than generating novel action sequences. This paper provides the first systematic zero-shot evaluation of frontier LLMs as goal recognisers on key classical PDDL benchmarks. Our results show that LLM competence on goal recognition is uneven: some models scale with evidence and approach landmark-based accuracy at full observations, while others remain anchored to world-knowledge priors regardless of how much evidence accumulates. Qualitative analysis of model reasoning traces reveals that this divergence reflects a fundamental difference in evidence integration rather than domain familiarity. These findings position goal recognition as a principled benchmark for the foundational planning knowledge of LLMs.

</details>


### [129] [Belief Engine: Configurable and Inspectable Stance Dynamics in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15343)
*Joshua C. Yang,Maurice Flechtner,Damian Dailisan,Michiel A. Bakker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信念引擎（Belief Engine, BE），一个可审计的信念更新层，将智能体的信念建模为关于命题的证据状态，并通过对数几率更新规则（受证据吸收率u和先验锚定度a控制）实现立场变化。在多种基座LLM上的参数扫描表明该方法能可靠塑造立场动态，并在人类辩论数据集DEBATE上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体在模拟协商、冲突解决等多轮交互时，生成的对话记录无法揭示立场变化的根本原因（如证据采纳、锚定、角色漂移、回音效应或提示/检索上下文变化），缺乏可审计性。

Method: BE将信念定义为关于命题的证据状态，将立场暴露为标量值。系统将论证提取到结构化记忆中，通过由证据吸收率u和先验锚定度a控制的对数几率规则更新立场，同时保留证据级别的更新轨迹。

Result: 在多个基座LLM上的参数扫描表明u和a能可靠地塑造立场动态；在DEBATE数据集上，BE对最终立场遵循提取证据的参与者重构效果最佳，而立场稳定或与证据相反的案例则指向锚定或证据流之外的因素。

Conclusion: BE为研究证据基础的 deliberation 提供了可配置基础设施，使得开放性、承诺、趋同和分歧可归因于显式的更新假设，而非隐藏的提示效应。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly used to simulate deliberative interactions such as negotiation, conflict resolution, and multi-turn opinion exchange. Yet generated transcripts often do not reveal why an agent's stance changes: movement may reflect evidence uptake, anchoring, role drift, echoing, or changed prompt and retrieval context. We introduce the Belief Engine (BE), an auditable belief-update layer that treats "belief" as an evidential state over a proposition and exposes it as scalar stance. BE extracts arguments into structured memory and updates stance with a log-odds rule controlled by evidence uptake u and prior anchoring a. Across multiple base LLMs, parameter sweeps show that these controls reliably shape stance dynamics while preserving an evidence-level update trail. On DEBATE, a human deliberation dataset with pre/post opinions, BE best reconstructs participants whose final stance follows extracted evidence; stable and evidence-opposed cases instead point to anchoring or factors outside the extracted evidence stream. BE provides configurable infrastructure for studying evidence-grounded deliberation, where openness, commitment, convergence, and disagreement can be tied to explicit update assumptions rather than hidden prompt effects.

</details>


### [130] [From LLM-Generated Conjectures to Lean Formalizations: Automated Polynomial Inequality Proving via Sum-of-Squares Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15445)
*Ruobing Zuo,Hanrui Zhao,Gaolei He,Zhengfeng Yang,Jianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NSPI是一个神经符号框架，结合LLM和符号计算，通过近似多项式平方和分解与符号精化，实现高效可扩展的多项式不等式自动证明，并在Lean中完成机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式不等式的自动证明面临可扩展性挑战：纯符号方法在高变量数或高次数下因代数操作复杂和中间表达式膨胀而扩展性差；LLM引导方法虽在竞赛型不等式上取得进展，但仍难以应对更大规模的问题。

Method: 提出NSPI框架：1) LLM提出近似多项式平方和(SOS)分解作为猜想；2) 通过符号计算精化该猜想，得到精确的多项式SOS表示，从而直接证明目标不等式；3) 在Lean中形式化验证证明，形成从启发式发现到机器可验证证明的端到端流水线。

Result: 在包含多达10个变量的多项式不等式基准测试上，NSPI方法展示了有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: NSPI通过神经符号融合策略，有效弥合了LLM的启发式探索能力与符号计算的严格推理能力之间的差距，为多项式不等式自动证明的可扩展性提供了新路径。

Abstract: Automated proving of polynomial inequalities is a fundamental challenge in automated mathematical reasoning, where rich algebraic structure and a rapidly growing certificate search space hinder scalability. Purely symbolic approaches provide strong guarantees but often scale poorly as the number of variables or the degree increases, due to expensive algebraic manipulations and rapidly growing intermediate expressions. In parallel, LLM-guided methods have made notable progress, particularly on competition-style inequalities with a small number of variables. To address the remaining scalability challenges, we propose NSPI, a neuro-symbolic framework that combines the complementary strengths of LLMs and symbolic computation for polynomial-inequality proving. Concretely, an LLM proposes a conjecture in the form of an approximate polynomial Sum-Of-Squares (SOS) decomposition; we refine it via symbolic computation to obtain an exact polynomial SOS representation, which directly proves the target inequality, and we further certify the proof in Lean, yielding an end-to-end pipeline from heuristic discovery to machine-checked proof. Experiments on challenging benchmarks involving polynomials with up to 10 variables demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [131] [CAPS: Cascaded Adaptive Pairwise Selection for Efficient Parallel Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15513)
*Fangzhou Lin,Shuo Xing,Peiran Li,Siyuan Yang,Qianwen Ge,Kazunori Yamada,Ziming Zhang,Haichong Zhang,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAPS提出了一种级联自适应成对选择框架，通过沿证据轴和分布轴非均匀分配验证器计算资源，在仅用25.4%的验证器token预算下，在14/20套评测中优于领先的成对验证器，并在全部20套中优于逐点自验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成对自验证方法每次判断都需要完整阅读两个候选解，且对问题中的所有比较均匀分配计算资源，导致大量冗余计算和验证器token开销过大。

Method: CAPS是纯推理框架，沿两个正交轴非均匀分配验证器计算：(1) 证据轴——自适应调整每个候选解被法官阅读的长度；(2) 分布轴——自适应调整比较在候选池中的分配方式。具体实现为四阶段级联架构（含可选救援子程序），并推导出闭式验证器token成本公式，使每个候选解的边际成本相比均匀全证据调度降低约一半。

Result: 在4个自验证模型和5个推理基准（代码+数学）上，CAPS在20套评测中14套优于领先成对验证器，仅使用其25.4%的验证器token预算；并在全部20套中优于逐点自验证。还提供了基于验证器在部分证据与全证据下准确率的可解释诊断指标。

Conclusion: CAPS通过自适应计算分配显著降低了成对自验证的计算成本，同时保持甚至提升性能，并提供了一套部署前的可解释性检查工具。

Abstract: Parallel reasoning, where a generator samples many candidate solutions and an aggregator selects the best, is one of the most effective forms of test-time scaling in large language models, and pairwise self-verification has become its strongest aggregation primitive. Yet pairwise verification carries a heavy cost: each judgment reads two complete solutions in full, and existing methods perform tens of such judgments per problem regardless of whether the comparison is informative. We introduce CAPS (Cascaded Adaptive Pairwise Selection), an inference-only framework that allocates verifier compute non-uniformly along two orthogonal axes: an evidence axis that adapts how much of each candidate the judge sees, and a distribution axis that adapts how comparisons are spread across the pool. CAPS instantiates these into a four-stage cascade with an optional rescue subroutine, and admits a closed-form verifier-token cost in which the per-candidate marginal cost is roughly halved relative to uniform full-evidence schedules. On four self-verifying models (Qwen3-14B, GPT-OSS-20B, Qwen3-4B-Instruct/Thinking) and five reasoning benchmarks spanning code (LiveCodeBench-v5/v6, CodeContests) and math (AIME 2025, HMMT 2025), CAPS outperforms the leading pairwise verifier on 14 of 20 suites while using 25.4% of its verifier-token budget on code, and outperforms pointwise self-verification on all 20. The trade-off suites admit an interpretable diagnostic in terms of the verifier's accuracy at partial versus full evidence, providing a concrete pre-deployment check for cascade suitability.

</details>


### [132] [DRS-GUI: Dynamic Region Search for Training-Free GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15542)
*Yichao Liu,Huawen Shen,Liu Yu,Shiyu Liu,Zeyu Chen,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DRS-GUI，一种无需训练的动态区域搜索框架，通过模拟人类视觉感知行为（聚焦、移动、分散）和基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的动作规划器，实现GUI元素精准定位，在ScreenSpot-Pro上提升14%的定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的GUI智能体在高分辨率屏幕截图中准确定位与指令相关的元素仍面临挑战，因为无关UI组件会造成干扰。受人类动态调整感知范围以定位复杂屏幕上任务相关区域的启发，提出一种无需训练的动态区域搜索方法。

Method: 提出DRS-GUI框架，包含：(1) 轻量级UI感知器，执行三种类人感知动作（聚焦Focus、移动Shift、分散Scatter）逐步探索界面并生成区域提议；(2) 基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的动作规划器，动态调度感知动作；(3) 区域质量奖励机制，评估和选择与指令高度相关的区域，有效剪枝冗余UI元素。

Result: 在ScreenSpot-Pro基准上，DRS-GUI使通用型及GUI专用型MLLM（Qwen2.5-VL-7B和UGround-V1-7B）的定位性能提升14%，显著增强了接地性能与泛化能力。

Conclusion: DRS-GUI作为一种无需训练的动态区域搜索框架，可无缝集成到现有MLLMs中，通过模拟人类视觉感知策略有效提升GUI元素定位的准确性和泛化性。

Abstract: GUI agents powered by Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capability in understanding and executing user instructions. However, accurately grounding instruction-relevant elements from high-resolution screenshots cluttered with irrelevant UI components remains challenging for existing approaches. Inspired by how humans dynamically adjust their perceptual scope to locate task-related regions on complex screens, we propose DRS-GUI, a training-free dynamic region search framework for GUI grounding that can be seamlessly integrated into existing MLLMs. DRS-GUI introduces a lightweight UI Perceptor that performs three human-like perceptual actions (Focus, Shift, and Scatter) to progressively explore the interface and generate region proposals. To dynamically schedule these actions, we further design an Action Planner based on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). A region quality reward is employed to evaluate and select the highly instruction-relevant region, efficiently pruning redundant UI elements. Experiments demonstrate that DRS-GUI yields a 14\% improvement on ScreenSpot-Pro for general and GUI-specific MLLMs (Qwen2.5-VL-7B and UGround-V1-7B), significantly enhancing grounding performance and generalization.

</details>


### [133] [Position: Artificial Intelligence Needs Meta Intelligence -- the Case for Metacognitive AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15567)
*Sergei Chuprov,Richard D. Lange,Leon Reznik,Paulo Shakarian,Raman Zatsarenko,Dmitrii Korobeinikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将元认知（metacognition）作为AI系统设计的一般性原则，通过让系统监控自身状态并根据问题难度或错误成本合理分配资源，以提升AI的准确性、安全性和效率。通过在联邦学习场景中的案例研究和开发的软件框架，展示了这些原则的可实践性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统缺乏对自身认知状态的感知和资源分配的灵活性，导致在准确率、安全性和效率方面存在局限。作者希望借鉴心理学和认知科学中的元认知策略，以及资源理性AI的思想，为AI设计提供更具自适应能力的原则性方法。

Method: 1）从心理学和认知科学中提取元认知策略；2）结合资源理性AI的过往工作；3）识别将这些策略嵌入AI设计的挑战和开放问题；4）通过联邦学习案例验证原则的有效性；5）开发一个软件框架供社区设计、部署和实验元认知能力增强的AI应用。

Result: 在联邦学习场景中，元认知原则提升了学习效率、有效性和安全性。同时，开发了一个可供社区使用的软件框架，支持元认知增强AI应用的设计与部署。

Conclusion: 元认知可以作为AI设计的一般性原则，帮助构建更准确、安全和高效的AI系统。该原则具有跨领域适用性，但仍有待解决的理论和实现问题。

Abstract: This position paper argues for metacognition as a general design principle for creating more accurate, secure, and efficient AI. The metacognitive solution involves systems monitoring their own states and judiciously allocating resources depending on each problem instance's difficulty or cost of mistakes. Drawing inspiration both from past work on resource-rational AI and from well-documented metacognitive strategies in psychology and cognitive science, we identify specific challenges in embedding these strategies into AI design and highlight open theoretical and implementation problems. We showcase these principles through a tangible example of improved learning efficiency, effectiveness, and security in a Federated Learning (FL) case study. We show how these principles can be translated into practice with a novel software framework developed specifically to allow the community to design, deploy, and experiment with metacognition-enabled AI applications.

</details>


### [134] [See Before You Code: Learning Visual Priors for Spatially Aware Educational Animation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15585)
*Yuejia Li,Ke He,Junheng Li,Shutong Chen,Jingkang Xia,Zhiyue Su,Junchi Zhang,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniManim框架，通过共享场景状态、显式视觉规划、结构化渲染后诊断和局部修复，解决大模型生成教育动画代码时出现的视觉缺陷问题。其中Vision Agent通过粗到细的边界框去噪和插值感知优化预测稀疏关键帧布局，在EduRequire-500数据集上显著提升了渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的动画代码在渲染后常出现元素重叠、对齐错误和动画连续性断裂等视觉缺陷，且这些缺陷无法仅从代码层面检测，必须在执行渲染后才能发现。现有方法缺乏渲染反馈感知能力，无法有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出OmniManim框架，核心包括：(1) 共享场景状态维持一致性；(2) 显式视觉规划（Vision Agent），通过粗到细的边界框去噪和插值感知目标优化预测稀疏关键帧布局；(3) 结构化后渲染诊断检测缺陷；(4) 局部化修复机制。同时构建了ManimLayout-1K和EduRequire-500两个数据集及可复现的评估协议。

Result: 在EduRequire-500数据集上，OmniManim在可执行性、教学质量、视觉质量和效率方面均优于单模型基线和现有多智能体框架。消融实验验证了显式视觉规划（尤其是粗粒度空间先验、边界框细化和插值感知优化）对性能提升的核心作用。

Conclusion: 将动画生成问题形式化为渲染反馈感知的约束代码生成，并验证了显式视觉规划结合渲染后诊断与修复是解决该问题的有效范式。

Abstract: Large language models can generate executable code for educational animations, but the resulting renders often exhibit visual defects, including element overlap, misalignment, and broken animation continuity. These defects cannot be reliably detected from the code alone and become apparent only after execution. We formalize this problem as render-feedback-aware constrained code generation: given a natural language specification, the model must generate executable code whose rendered output satisfies structured quality criteria that can be evaluated only after rendering. To address this problem, we introduce OmniManim, a render-feedback-aware educational animation generation framework built around a shared scene state, explicit visual planning, structured post-render diagnostics, and localized repair. Within OmniManim, the Vision Agent is a task-specific visual planning module: it predicts sparse keyframe layouts with coarse-to-fine bounding-box denoising and optimizes an interpolation-aware objective to reduce intermediate-frame failures induced by downstream animation interpolation. We further construct two datasets, ManimLayout-1K and EduRequire-500, and provide a reproducible evaluation protocol covering executability, instructional quality, visual quality, and efficiency. On EduRequire-500, OmniManim improves measured render quality over both single-model baselines and existing multi-agent frameworks. Systematic ablation studies further verify that explicit visual planning, especially its coarse spatial prior, bounding-box refinement, and interpolation-aware optimization, is central to these gains.

</details>


### [135] [TopoEvo: A Topology-Aware Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Framework for Root Cause Analysis in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15611)
*Junle Wang,Xingchuang Liao,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoEvo是一个拓扑感知的自进化多智能体框架，通过指标正交多模态对齐、向量量化离散化和假设-证据-测试工作流，解决微服务根因分析中多模态噪声、级联传播和拓扑漂移三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务根因分析面临三个核心挑战：(i) 多模态可观测数据（指标、日志、追踪）存在噪声和异构性；(ii) 级联故障传播放大下游症状；(iii) 自动扩缩容和滚动更新导致的非平稳拓扑漂移。现有LLM-based RCA智能体存在拓扑感知缺失和症状放大偏差问题，错误地将根因归因于显著的下游受害者。

Method: TopoEvo包含四个核心组件：(1) MOMA（指标正交多模态对齐）：将指标嵌入分解到互补子空间，并通过对比学习对齐日志和追踪，减少模态冗余和稀疏性；(2) VQ（向量量化）：将拓扑增强状态离散化为可审计的症状令牌，构建症状词典用于可靠检索和证据溯源；(3) HET（假设-证据-测试）多智能体工作流：显式验证传播一致性解释，区分初始异常与放大后的下游症状；(4) 自进化机制：刷新分层事件记忆，并利用高置信度伪标签进行保守的测试时自适应，保持拓扑漂移下的鲁棒性。

Result: 该方法能有效克服症状放大偏差，在多模态噪声和拓扑动态变化环境中实现更准确的根因定位，提供可审计的证据溯源。

Conclusion: TopoEvo通过结合图表示学习与结构化拓扑约束推理，为微服务根因分析提供了一种鲁棒的、可解释的解决方案，能够应对实际生产环境中的拓扑漂移和级联传播问题。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) in microservices is challenging due to (i) noisy and heterogeneous multimodal observability (metrics, logs, traces), (ii) cascading failure propagation that amplifies downstream symptoms, and (iii) non-stationary topology drift induced by autoscaling and rolling updates. Recent LLM-based RCA agents can generate tool-grounded explanations, yet they often remain topology-agnostic and suffer from \emph{symptom-amplification bias}, misattributing the root cause to salient downstream victims. We propose \textbf{TopoEvo}, a topology-aware self-evolving multi-agent framework that couples graph representation learning with structured, topology-constrained reasoning. TopoEvo first introduces \emph{Metric-orthogonal Multimodal Alignment} (MOMA), which decomposes metric embeddings into complementary subspaces and contrastively aligns logs and traces to reduce modality redundancy and sparsity, yielding stable node representations for graph encoding. It then applies \emph{Vector Quantization} (VQ) to discretize topology-enhanced states into auditable \emph{symptom tokens} with a symptom lexicon, enabling reliable retrieval and token-level evidence grounding. On top of these discrete topology cues, TopoEvo performs a multi-agent \emph{Hypothesis--Evidence--Test} (HET) workflow to explicitly verify propagation-consistent explanations and separate initiating anomalies from amplified downstream symptoms. Finally, a \emph{Self-Evolving Mechanism} refreshes hierarchical incident memory and performs conservative test-time adaptation with high-confidence pseudo-labels to maintain robustness under drift.

</details>


### [136] [ColPackAgent: Agent-Skill-Guided Hard-Particle Monte Carlo Workflows for Colloidal Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15625)
*Lijie Ding,Changwoo Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ColPackAgent是一个基于MCP协议和智能体技能的自主胶体堆积蒙特卡洛模拟框架，支持交互式、端到端和自动研究三种运行模式，并在多种LLM上进行了17阶段工作流的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型智能体缺乏专用模拟工具和工作流指令，只能描述而非可靠执行胶体堆积模拟工作流，因此需要将领域Python包与MCP工具和可移植智能体技能配对，将模拟工具包转化为智能体辅助的研究工作流。

Method: 构建colpack Python包并通过MCP工具服务器暴露HOOMD-blue硬粒子蒙特卡洛功能，设计四阶段工作流合约编码为智能体技能，支持三种运行模式（交互式人类反馈、端到端提示、遵循程序文件的自动研究），并在17个阶段特定提示下对面板级LLM进行基准测试。

Result: 在立方体粒子3D堆积、圆盘与胶囊二元体系2D堆积、以及2D硬圆盘冻结转变自动研究等多个示例中成功演示；通过17阶段基准测试评估了不同模型在设置、规划和分析工作流中的可靠性差异。

Conclusion: 将领域Python包与MCP工具和可移植智能体技能配对，是将模拟工具包转变为智能体辅助研究工作流的实用途径。

Abstract: We introduce ColPackAgent, an agent framework that autonomously runs Monte Carlo simulations of colloidal packing through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool server and an agent skill, whether as a standalone agent or inside an existing agent system. By harnessing the MCP server and agent skill, ColPackAgent executes a structured workflow for colloidal packing simulations, which are central to studies of phase behavior, self-assembly, and materials design. Without dedicated simulation tools and workflow instructions, general-purpose Large Language Model (LLM) agents tend to describe such workflows rather than execute them reliably. The MCP server exposes a custom-built colpack Python package that wraps HOOMD-blue hard-particle Monte Carlo, and the skill encodes a four-stage workflow contract. ColPackAgent can carry out the workflow interactively with human feedback, autonomously from an end-to-end prompt, or as autoresearch following a provided program file. We demonstrate the system in different modes with several colloidal packing simulation examples such as cube particles in 3D, a binary system of disks and capsules in 2D, and the 2D hard-disk freezing transition using autoresearch. We also compare model performance on this workflow across a panel of LLMs with 17 stage-specific prompts. This benchmark provides a stage-level check of how reliably different models follow the setup, planning, and analysis workflow. Together, these results show that pairing a domain Python package with MCP tools and a portable agent skill provides a practical route for turning a simulation toolkit into an agent-assisted research workflow.

</details>


### [137] [Nudging Beyond the Comfort Zone: Efficient Strategy-Guided Exploration for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15726)
*Chanuk Lee,Sangwoo Park,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NudgeRL框架，通过策略引导（Strategy Nudging）实现结构化、多样性驱动的探索，在RLVR中显著提升了推理能力。该方法无需昂贵的预言机监督，通过分解奖励信号和蒸馏目标高效利用多样化轨迹，在多个数学基准上超越了使用8倍采样预算的GRPO和依赖特权信息的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR方法受限于探索效率——策略只能从已采样的轨迹中学习。增加rollout数量虽能缓解但计算成本过高，而现有修改优化目标的方法对探索过程的控制有限。

Method: 提出NudgeRL框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Strategy Nudging——在每次rollout中注入轻量级的策略级上下文条件，以产生多样化的推理轨迹，无需昂贵的外部监督；(2) 统一优化目标——将奖励信号分解为上下文间和上下文内两部分，并引入蒸馏目标将探索到的行为迁移回基础策略。

Result: NudgeRL在五个具有挑战性的数学基准上，平均表现优于使用高达8倍rollout预算的标准GRPO，也超越了依赖预言机指导的RL基线方法。

Conclusion: 结构化、上下文驱动的探索可以作为一种高效且可扩展的替代方案，既能替代暴力增加rollout的缩放策略，也能替代基于特权信息的可行性导向方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a scalable paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. However, its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by exploration: the policy can only improve on trajectories it has already sampled. While increasing the number of rollouts alleviates this issue, such brute-force scaling is computationally expensive, and existing approaches that modify the optimization objective provide limited control over what is explored. In this work, we propose NudgeRL, a framework for structured and diversity-driven exploration in RLVR. Our approach introduces Strategy Nudging, which conditions each rollout on lightweight, strategy-level contexts to induce diverse reasoning trajectories without relying on expensive oracle supervision. To effectively learn from such structured exploration, we further propose a unified objective, which decomposes the reward signal into inter- and intra-context components and incorporates a distillation objective to transfer discovered behaviors back to the base policy. Empirically, NudgeRL outperforms standard GRPO with up to 8 times larger rollout budgets, while outperforming oracle-guided RL baseline on average across five challenging math benchmarks. These results demonstrate that structured, context-driven exploration can serve as an efficient and scalable alternative to both brute-force rollout scaling and feasibility-oriented methods based on privileged information. Our code is available at https://github.com/tally0818/NudgeRL.

</details>


### [138] [Can We Trust AI-Inferred User States. A Psychometric Framework for Validating the Reliability of Users States Classification by LLMs in Operational Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15734)
*Izabella Krzeminska,Michal Butkiewicz,Ewa Komkowska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实证检验发现，大型语言模型用于评估用户状态的指标在个体分数层面缺乏稳定性，213个指标中仅31个满足可靠性标准，并提出了可复现的评估框架以支持更负责任的适应性AI系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有会话式与自适应系统依赖大语言模型评估用户状态，但该做法的前提假设——即评估指标在个体分数层面具有稳定性和可解释性——从未经过严格验证。本文旨在实证检验这一假设的成立性。

Method: 采用复制评估程序，在三种双模态大语言模型（GPT-4o audio、Gemini 2.0 Flash、Gemini 2.5 Flash）上测试了广泛指标集的可重复性，分别进行了个体分数信度分析和聚合信度分析。

Result: 213个指标中仅31个满足可靠性标准；个体分数层面缺乏稳定性，使得这些分数无法在实时自适应系统中作为用户状态指标；但个体层面不稳定的指标在事后分析中仍可保持分析效用。

Conclusion: 指标可靠性不能被默认视为解释性领域的固有属性；提出了一个可复现的评估框架，支持对指标适用性进行可量化的评估，推动更负责任的AI自适应系统设计，其中结果解释需要明确的信度验证并随时间持续监测。

Abstract: The use of large language models to assess user states in conversational and adaptive systems is based on the assumption that the metrics used for such assessment are stable and interpretable at the level of individual scores. This paper empirically tests this assumption, focusing on the psychometric reliability of artificial intelligence (AI) measures of user states.
  This study employed replication evaluation procedures to assess the repeatability of a broad set of metrics across three different bimodal large language models (GPT-4o audio, Gemini 2.0 Flash, Gemini 2.5 Flash). Analyses include both individual score reliability and aggregated reliability, allowing us to distinguish metrics potentially useful for real-time adaptation from those that retain their value only in aggregated analyses.
  The results demonstrate that metric reliability cannot be considered a default property in interpretive domains. The lack of stability at the level of individual scores precludes the interpretation of such scores as indicators of user state in real-time adaptive systems, even if these metrics demonstrate stability after aggregation. At the same time, the study indicates that individually unstable metrics can retain analytical utility in post-hoc studies, identifying rules governing interactions and their relationships with user experience parameters such as satisfaction, trust, and engagement.
  The main contribution of this work, besides quantifying the severity of the problem (only 31 of 213 metrics met the criteria), is the proposal of a replicable evaluation framework, enabling measurable evaluations of metric applicability. This approach supports more responsible AI design of adaptive systems, in which the interpretation of results requires explicit validation of reliability and monitoring for violations over time.

</details>


### [139] [SaaS-Bench: Can Computer-Use Agents Leverage Real-World SaaS to Solve Professional Workflows?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15777)
*Kean Shi,Zihang Li,Tianyi Ma,Zengji Tu,Jialong Wu,Xinbo Xu,Qingyao Yang,Ruoyu Wu,Weichu Xie,Ming Wu,Jason Zeng,Michael Heinrich,Elvis Zhang,Liang Chen,Kuan Li,Baobao Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SaaS-Bench，一个基于23个真实SaaS系统、包含106个长周期任务的基准测试，用于评估计算机使用智能体在专业工作流程中的能力。实验显示，最强LLM智能体的端到端任务完成率不足4%，暴露了规划、状态跟踪和错误恢复等方面的严重局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页和GUI智能体基准测试多基于简化设置、孤立任务或短周期交互，难以评估智能体在真实专业工作流中的能力。SaaS环境因其动态系统状态、跨应用协调、领域专业知识和长周期依赖等特性，是评估CUA能力的自然选择。

Method: 构建了23个可部署的SaaS系统，覆盖六个专业领域，包含106个基于真实工作场景的任务。任务需要长周期执行，涵盖纯文本和多模态设置，使用加权验证检查点评估任务完成度和部分进度。

Result: 代表性LLM智能体在SaaS-Bench上表现困难，最强模型端到端任务完成率不足4%，在规划、状态跟踪、跨应用上下文维护和错误恢复方面存在明显局限。

Conclusion: SaaS-Bench揭示了当前CUA在复杂专业工作流中的严重不足，为未来智能体在长周期、跨应用、动态环境中的能力提升提供了有价值的评估基准。

Abstract: Computer-Using Agents (CUAs) are rapidly extending large language models (LLMs) beyond text-based reasoning toward action execution in more complex environments, such as web browsers and graphical user interfaces (GUIs). However, existing web and GUI agent benchmarks often rely on simplified settings, isolated tasks, or short-horizon interactions, making it difficult to assess capabilities of agents in realistic professional workflows. Software-as-a-Service (SaaS) environments are a natural choice for CUA evaluation, as they host a large share of modern digital work and naturally involve dynamic system states, cross-application coordination, domain-specific knowledge, and long-horizon dependencies. To this end, we introduce SaaS-Bench, a benchmark built on 23 deployable SaaS systems across six professional domains, containing 106 tasks grounded in realistic work scenarios. These tasks require long-horizon execution, cover both text-only and multimodal settings, and are evaluated with weighted verification checkpoints that measure strict task completion and partial progress. Experiments show that representative LLM-based agents struggle on SaaS-Bench, with even the strongest model completing fewer than 4% of tasks end-to-end, exposing limitations in planning, state tracking, cross-application context maintenance, and error recovery. Code are available at https://github.com/UniPat-AI/SaaS-Bench for reproduction.

</details>


### [140] [Agentic Discovery of Neural Architectures: AIRA-Compose and AIRA-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15871)
*Alberto Pepe,Chien-Yu Lin,Despoina Magka,Bilge Acun,Yannan Nellie Wu,Anton Protopopov,Carole-Jean Wu,Yoram Bachrach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出双框架方法（AIRA-Compose和AIRA-Design），使LLM智能体能够自主设计超越标准Transformer的基础模型架构。AIRA-Compose通过11个智能体搜索高级架构，发现了14种架构（含Transformer和Transformer-Mamba混合变体），在1B规模预训练后一致优于Llama 3.2等基线；AIRA-Design通过20个智能体设计新型注意力机制，在Long Range Arena上接近人类SOTA水平。研究表明AI智能体可自主发现匹配或超越手工设计的架构与算法优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM智能体能否通过递归自我改进，自主设计超越标准Transformer的基础模型架构，以推动下一代基础模型的自动发现。

Method: 提出双框架方法：AIRA-Compose（11个智能体，24小时预算，从百万参数候选架构外推至3B规模，评估14种架构）和AIRA-Design（20个智能体，编写新型注意力机制和训练脚本）。

Result: AIRAformer-D和AIRAhybrid-D在1B规模下游任务准确率分别比Llama 3.2高2.4%和3.8%；AIRAformer-C扩展速度比Llama 3.2快54%、比Composer最佳Transformer快71%；AIRAhybrid-C比Nemotron-2快23%、比Composer最佳混合模型快37%；Long Range Arena上接近人类SOTA（差距2.3%-2.6%）；Autoresearch上Greedy Opus 4.5达0.968验证bits-per-byte。

Conclusion: AI智能体可以自主发现匹配或超越手工设计的架构和算法优化，为发现下一代基础模型建立了强大范式，标志着向递归自我改进迈出了明确一步。

Abstract: Toward recursive self-improvement, we investigate LLM agents autonomously designing foundation models beyond standard Transformers. We introduce a dual-framework approach: AIRA-Compose for high-level architecture search, and AIRA-Design for low-level mechanistic implementation. AIRA-Compose uses 11 agents to explore fundamental computational primitives under a 24-hour budget. Agents evaluate million-parameter candidates, extrapolating top designs to 350M, 1B, and 3B scales. This yields 14 architectures across two families: AIRAformers (Transformer-based) and AIRAhybrids (Transformer-Mamba). Pre-trained at 1B scale, these consistently outperform Llama 3.2 and Composer-found baselines. On downstream tasks, AIRAformer-D and AIRAhybrid-D improve accuracy by 2.4% and 3.8% over Llama 3.2. Furthermore, AIRA-Compose finds models with highly efficient scaling frontiers: AIRAformer-C scales 54% and 71% faster than Llama 3.2 and Composer's best Transformer, while AIRAhybrid-C outscales Nemotron-2 by 23% and Composer's best hybrid by 37%. AIRA-Design tasks 20 agents with writing novel attention mechanisms for long-range dependencies and high-performing training scripts. On the Long Range Arena benchmark, agent-designed architectures reach within 2.3% and 2.6% of human state-of-the-art on document matching and text classification. On the Autoresearch benchmark, Greedy Opus 4.5 achieves 0.968 validation bits-per-byte under a fixed time budget, surpassing the published minimum. Together, these frameworks show AI agents can autonomously discover architectures and algorithmic optimizations matching or surpassing hand-designed baselines. This establishes a powerful paradigm for discovering next-generation foundation models, marking a clear step toward recursive self-improvement.

</details>


### [141] [Imperfect World Models are Exploitable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15960)
*Logan Mondal Bhamidipaty,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,David Abel,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了强化学习中世界模型可被利用（exploitation）的精确定义，类比奖励黑客（reward hacking）但发现其必然性证明不直接迁移，进而建立了奖励黑客与模型利用之间的形式化桥梁，证明了在大策略集中模型利用不可避免，并引入松弛概念推导了安全规划界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对奖励黑客有形式化定义，但对世界模型被利用（即模型误导策略选择）缺乏严格定义和理论分析，作者希望填补这一空白并建立两者间的形式化联系。

Method: 首先类比奖励黑客定义提出模型利用的定义；然后分析奖励黑客必然性证明为何不适用于模型利用；接着发展统一的通用理论，证明在大策略集上模型利用不可避免（且奖励黑客作为特例）；最后引入松弛的利用概念并推导可避免的安全规划范围。

Result: 模型利用在大策略集上本质上不可避免；有限策略集上保证无奖励黑客的条件无法推广到无模型利用；引入松弛利用概念后可以限定安全规划范围。

Conclusion: 建立了奖励黑客与模型利用的形式化理论桥接，清晰揭示了在基于世界模型的规划中安全性的理论极限。

Abstract: We propose a novel definition of model exploitation in reinforcement learning. Informally, a world model is exploitable if it implies that one policy should be strictly preferred over another while the environment's true transition model implies the reverse. We analogize our definition with a prior characterization of reward hacking but show that the associated proof of inevitability does not transfer to exploitation. To overcome this obstruction, we develop a general theory of reward hacking and model exploitation that proves that exploitation is essentially unavoidable on large policy sets and yields the corresponding claim for hacking as a special case. Unfortunately, we also find that the conditions that guarantee unhackability in finite policy sets have no counterpart that precludes exploitation. Consequently, we introduce a relaxed notion of exploitation and derive a safe horizon within which it can be avoided. Taken together, our results establish a formal bridge between reward hacking and model exploitation and elucidate the limits of safe planning in world models.

</details>


### [142] [PAGER: Bridging the Semantic-Execution Gap in Point-Precise Geometric GUI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15963)
*Jingxuan Wei,Xi Bai,Shan Liu,Caijun Jia,Zheng Sun,Xinglong Xu,Siyuan Li,Linzhuang Sun,Bihui Yu,Conghui He,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAGE Bench基准测试和PAGER智能体框架，解决需要点级精度的GUI几何构建任务。PAGER通过依赖结构规划和像素级执行，并采用像素级监督微调与精度对齐强化学习，在点精确GUI控制任务上取得显著提升（任务成功率提升4.1倍，步骤成功率从9%提升至62%以上）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体采用区域容错范式，忽视了需要对连续画布空间进行点精确操作的几何构建场景（如CAD绘图）。这类任务中局部坐标误差会通过几何原语的拓扑依赖关系级联传播，导致下游目标畸变和最终构建失效，亟需专门的方法和基准来应对这一精度敏感型GUI任务。

Method: 提出PAGER智能体框架，将几何构建任务分解为依赖结构规划（dependency-structured planning）和像素级执行（pixel-level execution）两个阶段。采用像素级监督微调（pixel-grounded supervised tuning）建立可执行的行动语法，并通过精度对齐强化学习（precision-aligned reinforcement learning）利用状态条件几何反馈缓解展开偏差（exposure bias）。同时构建PAGE Bench基准，包含4,906个问题和超过224K个过程监督的像素级GUI动作。

Result: 实验发现显著的"语义-执行鸿沟"（Semantic-Execution Gap）：通用多模态模型动作类型准确率超过88%但任务成功率低于6%。PAGER将任务成功率提升至最强通用基线的4.1倍，将步骤成功率从GUI专精智能体的9%以下提升至62%以上，达到点精确GUI控制的新状态最优。

Conclusion: 页面几何构建任务对点级精度敏感，传统区域容错范式不足以胜任。依赖感知的智能体设计结合像素级监督和精度对齐强化学习，能够有效弥合语义理解与精确执行之间的鸿沟，为精度敏感型GUI任务开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have significantly advanced GUI agents, enabling executable interaction across web, mobile, and desktop interfaces. Yet these gains largely rely on a forgiving region-tolerant paradigm, where many nearby pixels inside the same component remain valid. Precise geometric construction breaks this assumption: actions must land on points in continuous canvas space rather than tolerant regions. Because geometric primitives carry ontological dependencies, a local coordinate error can induce cascading topological failures that distort downstream objects and invalidate the final construction. We identify this regime as precision-sensitive GUI tasks, requiring point-level accuracy, geometry-aware verification, and robustness to dependency-driven error propagation. To benchmark it, we introduce PAGE Bench, with 4,906 problems and over 224K process-supervised, pixel-level GUI actions. We further propose PAGER, a topology-aware agent that decomposes construction into dependency-structured planning and pixel-level execution. Pixel-grounded supervised tuning establishes executable action grammar, while precision-aligned reinforcement learning mitigates rollout-induced exposure bias through state-conditioned geometric feedback. Experiments reveal a pronounced Semantic-Execution Gap: general multimodal models can exceed 88% action type accuracy yet remain below 6% task success. PAGER closes this gap, delivering 4.1x higher task success than the strongest evaluated general baseline and raising step success rate from below 9% for GUI-specialized agents to over 62%, establishing a new state of the art for point-precise GUI control.

</details>


### [143] [Deterministic Event-Graph Substrates as World Models for Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15967)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出事件图基底（event-graph substrates）——一种将智能体状态表示为只追加的RDF三元组日志、并通过结构化干预词汇对日志进行分叉来回答反事实查询的世界模型。该形式化方法在三元组级别可检查，支持精确反事实，且无需学习组件即可跨领域迁移。论文证明了解释性查询与反事实查询之间的对偶性，将两者归约为相同的因果祖先遍历。在CLEVRER数据集（n=75,618）上，1,400行的DSL解释器在领域无关的基底运行时上全面超越了NS-DR符号基线（四个问题类别分别提升9.89、20.26、17.65和0.80个百分点），在描述性和解释性问题上超越参数化ALOE基线，但在预测性和反事实问题上略逊。论文还提出了twin-EventLog基准——包含500个规格说明的Smallville反事实基准，该基底在该基准上的联合准确率超过完整上下文的Llama-3.1-8B达18.80个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在可检查性、精确反事实推理和跨领域迁移方面存在不足。参数化模型缺乏对内部表示的细粒度检查能力，而符号方法往往难以泛化。作者希望设计一种既具备符号系统的可解释性和精确性，又能跨领域迁移的世界模型形式化方法。

Method: 提出事件图基底(event-graph substrates)形式化框架：(1) 将智能体状态表示为只追加的RDF三元组事件日志；(2) 通过结构化干预词汇对日志分叉实现反事实查询；(3) 利用解释性与反事实查询之间的对偶性，将两者归约为因果祖先遍历算法。实现上构建了1,400行的CLEVRER-DSL解释器运行在领域无关的基底运行时上。

Result: 在CLEVRER数据集（n=75,618）上，基底超越NS-DR符号基线所有四个问题类别（+9.89, +20.26, +17.65, +0.80个百分点），在描述性和解释性上超越参数化ALOE基线，但在预测性和反事实方面略逊。在twin-EventLog基准（500个规格说明）上，基底联合准确率超越完整上下文的Llama-3.1-8B达18.80个百分点。

Conclusion: 事件图基底是一种可检查、支持精确反事实推理且跨领域可迁移的世界模型形式化框架。其优势在于无需学习组件、完全可解释，并在多个基准上取得了具有竞争力的结果。局限性在于反事实和预测性推理能力尚不及最优参数化模型，但为符号与神经方法的结合提供了新方向。

Abstract: We study event-graph substrates: a class of world models that represent agent state as an append-only log of typed RDF triples and answer counterfactual queries by forking the log under a structured intervention vocabulary. Substrates are inspectable at the triple level, support exact counterfactuals, and transfer across domains without learned components. We formalize the class, prove a duality between explanatory and counterfactual queries that reduces both to the same causal-ancestor traversal, and evaluate a 1,400-line CLEVRER-DSL interpreter atop a domain-agnostic substrate runtime at full CLEVRER validation scale (n=75,618). The substrate exceeds the NS-DR symbolic oracle on all four per-question categories (by 9.89, 20.26, 17.65, and 0.80 percentage points), and exceeds the parametric ALOE baseline on descriptive and explanatory while lagging on predictive and counterfactual. We also introduce twin-EventLog, a 500-specification Park-canonical Smallville counterfactual benchmark on which the substrate exceeds Llama-3.1-8B with full context by 18.80 points joint accuracy.

</details>


### [144] [Learning Bilevel Policies over Symbolic World Models for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15975)
*Dillon Z. Chen,Till Hofmann,Toryn Q. Klassen,Sheila A. McIlraith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BISON系统，通过双层策略（高层符号策略+底层模仿学习策略）结合符号抽象与模仿学习的优势，实现高效的长时域规划，在MetaWorld基准测试中优于VLA和端到端方法，且计算效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习在底层连续环境中的精细操作上表现优异，但难以独立生成长期规划；而高层符号抽象虽能实现可解释的长时域规划，却缺乏对底层控制的精细建模。因此需要将两者结合以互补优势。

Method: 提出双层策略框架(π^hl, π^ll)：π^ll是从底层演示中学习的神经策略，负责操控与连续控制；π^hl是基于底层演示的符号抽象与归纳泛化构建的高层符号策略，负责长时域规划。两者结合为BISON系统。

Result: 在扩展的MetaWorld基准测试中，BISON在长时域和更多物体数量的场景下优于VLA和端到端方法；训练和推理的时空效率更高；忽略底层执行时，高层策略可在1分钟内解决包含10,000个相关物体的高层规划问题。

Conclusion: 结合底层模仿学习与高层符号抽象的双层策略框架能有效提升具身智能体在长时域规划问题上的泛化能力和计算效率，是目前优于纯端到端方法的可行方案。

Abstract: We tackle the challenge of building embodied AI agents that can reliably solve long-horizon planning problems. Imitation learning from demonstrations has shown itself to be effective in training robots to solve a diversity of complex tasks requiring fine motor control and manipulation over low-level (LL), continuous environments. Yet, it remains a difficult endeavour to generate long-horizon plans from imitation learning alone. In contrast, high-level (HL), symbolic abstractions facilitate efficient and interpretable long-horizon planning. We propose to combine the strengths of LL imitation learning for manipulation and control, and HL symbolic abstractions for long-horizon planning. We realise this idea via \emph{bilevel policies} of the form $(π^{\mathrm{hl}}, π^{\mathrm{ll}})$, consisting of a neural policy $π^{\mathrm{ll}}$ learned from LL demonstrations, and an HL symbolic policy $π^{\mathrm{hl}}$ that is constructed from symbolic abstractions of the LL demonstrations combined with inductive generalisation. We implement these ideas in the BISON system. Experiments on extended MetaWorld benchmarks demonstrate that BISON generalises to long horizons and problems with greater numbers of objects than those solved by VLA and end-to-end methods, and is more time and memory efficient in training and inference. Notably, when ignoring LL execution, BISON's HL policies can solve HL problems with 10,000 relevant objects in under a minute. Project page: https://dillonzchen.github.io/bison

</details>


### [145] [Petri Net Induced Heuristic Search for Resource Constrained Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15983)
*Ido Lublin,Dor Atzmon,Izack Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将资源约束项目调度问题（RCPSP）建模为带资源的计时变迁Petri网的可达图上的最优搜索，使用相对延迟令牌使调度决策对应状态空间中的变迁触发，采用结合关键路径和资源下界的A*启发式搜索求解，并在PSPLIB基准测试上优于MIP基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCPSP求解方法（如MIP）在处理大规模问题时面临求解成功率低和耗时长的挑战，需要更高效的精确求解方法。

Method: 将RCPSP建模为计时变迁Petri网（TTPN with Resources），利用相对延迟令牌将调度决策转化为变迁触发；采用A*搜索，启发式函数结合关键路径下界和资源下界，并证明该启发式在令牌时间语义下是一致的。

Result: 在PSPLIB基准测试上，该方法在求解成功率和求解时间两方面均优于SCIP和CBC两种MIP基线；A*与MIP的性能退化维度正交：A*受资源紧度影响，MIP受问题规模影响，资源强度决定了何种求解器在规模扩大时更具优势。

Conclusion: 基于Petri网可达图和A*启发式搜索的RCPSP求解方法是一种有竞争力的精确求解范式，与MIP形成互补优势。

Abstract: We formulate the Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem (RCPSP) as optimal search over the reachability graph of a Timed Transition Petri Net with Resources, using relative-delay tokens so that scheduling decisions correspond to transition firings in the induced state space. We solve the resulting problem with $A^*$ guided by a heuristic that combines Critical Path and resource-based lower bounds, and prove that it is consistent under our token-based time semantics. Experiments on the PSPLIB benchmarks show that the approach outperforms strong exact Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MIP) baselines (SCIP, CBC) in both success rate and solve time. Per-instance analysis shows that heuristic search and MIP degrade along independent axes, resource tightness for $A^*$ and formulation size for MIP, with resource strength mediating which solver benefits from scale.

</details>


### [146] [ScreenSearch: Uncertainty-Aware OS Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16024)
*Michael Solodko,Justin Wagle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ScreenSearch系统，通过结构化的屏幕检索与去重，结合基于PUCT的模糊感知图搜索算法，解决桌面GUI智能体在部分可观测环境下的状态探索问题。系统在11个桌面应用中收集了超过100万张截图和3万个去重状态，揭示了新颖性与模糊性之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桌面GUI智能体面临部分可观测性问题：视觉相似的屏幕可能对应不同的底层工作流状态，局部合理的操作可能导致截然不同的结果。需要一种有效的状态探索方法来扩大可达边界并减少歧义。

Method: ScreenSearch系统包含两个核心层：(1) 结构化屏幕检索与去重层，将UIA树转换为位置感知的结构化特征，通过稀疏token搜索和元数据过滤器索引相关屏幕，并在多个VM工作节点间维护共享去重状态图；(2) 基于动作结果离群的模糊感知PUCT图搜索算法，当相似屏幕在相同动作签名下产生不同下一状态时，信号指示该状态需要进一步探测而非视为已解决。

Result: 在11个桌面应用中，系统收集了超过100万张截图和3万个去重状态，生成了具有显著跨应用和内部多样性的探索语料库。在固定回放切片上观察到清晰的新颖性-模糊性权衡：某些策略快速减少模糊性但鲜有发现新边界。消融实验表明更强的提议先验可显著改善唯一状态发现。

Conclusion: 状态身份识别、提议质量和模糊感知搜索在决定何时探测与何时提交时都很重要。仅减少模糊性不足以作为充分的探索目标。

Abstract: Desktop GUI agents operate under partial observability: visually similar screens can correspond to different underlying workflow states, so locally plausible actions can lead to sharply different outcomes. We frame this as a problem of computer/OS state exploration, where effective behavior requires both expanding the reachable frontier and reducing ambiguity before committing. We present ScreenSearch, a system that combines structural screen retrieval and deduplication with an ambiguity-aware PUCT graph-bandit for large-scale desktop exploration. The retrieval layer converts UIA trees into location-aware structural features, indexes related screens through sparse token search and metadata filters, and maintains a shared deduplicated state graph across VM workers. On top of this graph, we define a scalable ambiguity signal based on matched-action outcome dispersion. If similar screens produce different next states under the same action signature, the state should be probed further rather than treated as resolved. We use this signal together with frontier rewards to drive large-scale exploration and replay-start policy evaluation over the shared graph. Across 11 desktop applications, ScreenSearch collects over 1M screenshots and over 30K deduplicated states, yielding large exploration corpora with substantial cross-application and within-application diversity. On a fixed replay-start slice, we observe a clear novelty--ambiguity trade-off: some policies reduce ambiguity quickly while discovering little frontier. Ambiguity reduction alone is therefore not a sufficient exploration objective. Appendix ablations show that stronger proposal priors can materially improve unique-state discovery during corpus building. These results suggest that state identity, proposal quality, and ambiguity-aware search all matter when deciding when to probe and when to commit.

</details>


### [147] [Reasoners or Translators? Contamination-aware Evaluation and Neuro-Symbolic Robustness in Tax Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16052)
*Parisa Kordjamshidi,Samer Aslan,Madhavan Seshadri,Leslie Barrett,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过污染检测协议揭示了LLM在法律推理中存在数据污染导致的性能膨胀问题，并系统比较了纯神经方法与神经符号混合方法，发现后者在泛化性和可靠性上更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在法律推理任务中的表现究竟是源于真正的推理能力还是数据污染带来的虚假提升。

Method: 设计并实现了污染检测协议来评估LLM的可靠性；构建了包含案例和规则变体的新测试套件，系统比较了纯神经模型与将法规文本转换为形式化表示并交由符号求解器推理的神经符号混合系统。

Result: 发现数据污染会显著膨胀模型性能；法律推理本质上是组合性的；神经符号框架在泛化性和鲁棒性上优于纯神经模型。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法为法律AI提供了更可靠、更鲁棒的基础，在未见过场景上具有更好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have significantly enhanced automated legal reasoning. Yet, it remains unclear whether their performance reflects genuine legal reasoning ability or artifacts of data contamination. We present a comprehensive empirical study of tax law reasoning approaches and implement a contamination detection protocol to rigorously assess LLM reliability. We show that performance can be inflated by contamination. Building on this analysis, we conduct a systematic evaluation, comparing monolithic LLMs with hybrid systems that translate statutory text into formal representations and delegate inference to symbolic solvers. We build a novel test suite designed to probe generalization to unseen documents via case and rule variations. Our findings indicate that legal reasoning is inherently compositional and that neuro-symbolic frameworks offer a more reliable and robust foundation for legal AI, as well as improved generalization to unobserved situations.

</details>


### [148] [Sign-Separated Finite-Time Error Analysis of Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16103)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对常数步长Q-learning进行了符号分离的有限时间误差分析，通过将误差分解为正负分量，发现负误差受最优策略的LTI系统控制而收敛更快，正误差由线性切换系统控制，揭示了Q-learning动态中由max算子引起的非对称性，并联系到过估计现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q-learning的有限时间分析通常考虑整体误差范数，忽略了误差符号方向上的非对称动态特性。本文旨在揭示Q-learning中正负误差的不同演化机制，特别是max算子引入的偏差不对称性，并建立更精细的收敛边界。

Method: 将Q-learning误差动态建模为切换系统，将误差向量按分量符号分解为负部和正部。负部用固定最优策略下的LTI系统逼近作为下界，正部用线性切换系统控制，分别导出其指数收敛包络线。

Result: 负侧LTI证书的收敛速度不慢于正侧切换证书，且可能具有更快的指数包络；识别出Q-learning误差动态中max诱导的非对称性，其中正的逐动作误差会被Bellman max算子选择和传播，而负误差则受最优策略下界约束。

Conclusion: 该分析从符号分离角度解释了Q-learning过估计偏差的根源（正误差被max放大而负误差被抑制），并为确定性和随机常数步长递归均提供了有限时间界，为设计更稳定的强化学习算法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: This paper develops a sign-separated finite-time error analysis for constant step-size Q-learning. Starting from the switching-system representation, the error is decomposed into its componentwise negative and positive parts. The negative part is dominated by a lower comparison linear time-invariant (LTI) system associated with a fixed optimal policy, whereas the positive part is controlled by a linear switching system. The resulting bounds show that the negative-side LTI certificate is no slower than the positive-side switching certificate and may produce a faster exponential envelope.
